# Parity gaps, 7 November 2026

The first gap report of the parity harness (`docs/plans/parity-harness.md`), from the Parity workflow's run on commit c8860c66 of #347 (run 42314572677). Mode `report`: the gaps below do not fail a pull request yet.

## What this report says

- 3,814 Conductor entries: 703 matched to a catalogue state and element, 57 decided (D086), 3,054 gaps. Of the gaps, 891 are **unshown** (our UI source holds the same string, but no catalogue state draws it) and 2,163 are **missing** (our UI source does not hold it, so it is a gap in the product or a decision still to write).
- 1,209 ours-only strings on screen (256 distinct): wording of ours that no Conductor entry, template or sample value accounts for.
- 141 backend names of ours with no Conductor name matched to them.
- Layout: all 13 measured elements are within the tolerance at this commit.
- Flows: 5 of 7 drivable flows pass. Create a workspace stops after Create, and the catalogue logged an unscripted `git fetch`; Open settings from the palette never shows the settings navigation (not yet known whether the flow or the app is wrong). The three add-project paths are not drivable in the catalogue yet.
- Instrument check: passed. On 29e65165 the harness reports the sidebar footer's "Add a project" button and "Write something ..." as ours only, Conductor's composer placeholder as missing, and the tool-call row's padding, radius and height as off Conductor's. None of the three is reported on this commit.

## How to read the gap list

- Screens: a Conductor entry is placed by the `ui-map.md` or `docs/coverage/` section that cites the nearest bundle offset (within 20,000 characters); "Unplaced" means no citation is near. The team and hosted features D004 and D025 leave out sit mostly there and under the coverage audits' Cloud heading; they are gaps until someone maps them to those decisions in the map.
- An ours-only row lists every state that shows the string. A string seed data could not explain, such as a branch name our UI source also holds, shows here too.
- The backend gaps are mostly names our protocol, commands and tables chose for themselves; stage 2 will show which of them Conductor's frontend needs.
